Written to be deleted by the `shell-page-frame` migration — a symptom fix that says so. |
| 2026-08-14 | T-102 · the gate learns to see the defects it was written for | [2026-08-14-T-102-typography-gate-detection](sessions/2026-08/2026-08-14-T-102-typography-gate-detection.md) | **Four tasks — T-090, T-091, T-092, T-097 — each found their real defect by measuring a render *after* the gate reported the route clean.** That is a tooling defect, not four unlucky sessions. Three rules added to `check-typography.mjs`: `legacy-type-class-in-jsx`, whose fourteen class names are **derived by parsing the four frozen sheets** for blocks that are *only* typography (`tokens.css:219-230` → the twelve `.t-*` utilities, `saqeel-runtime.css:61` → `.sq-overline`, `saqeel-components.css:163` → `.id-code`) rather than invented; `legacy-type-token` for `--type-*` outside the frozen four, deliberately **not** exempting `components/saqeel/` because the authoring zone may declare typography but not from the *pre-SAQEEL* scale; and `inline-font-style` promoted to a **multiline** matcher — the rule was always correct and the engine was not, since patterns were tested line by line and `[^}]*` can never cross a newline. **Baseline 733 → 1846: +1142 newly detected, −29 from the concurrent T-101.** The number did not get worse, the instrument got honest. **WEB-014 §8's ratchet governs regressions, not detection** — otherwise every blind spot is permanent by construction, and the rule text should be amended. Found on **live** migrated code and scoring zero until today: `EmptyState.tsx:28` (44 consumers), `ContextualAiPanel.tsx` ×4 and `GeoMap.tsx:315` (18 consumers) all render **11.5px prose** — below the 14px floor — and `PlanningReadFailure.tsx:26` renders **12.5px in `ui-monospace`**, a **second typeface** §2.0 forbids. Detection deliberately stops at whole-token typography classes: the frozen sheets define ~180 classes carrying a font, but those are *component* classes and banning them is a migration decision, not a typography one. **Renumbered T-101 → T-102** on discovering a concurrent session holding that ID — the third collision after T-076 twice. **`npm run lint` does not exist in `package.json`**, so every session that ticked WEB-008 §3's lint gate ticked something it could not have run — the second missing script after `gate:retirement`. |
| 2026-08-14 | T-101 · the notification panel leaves the frozen sheets | [2026-08-14-T-101-notification-panel-rebuild](sessions/2026-08/2026-08-14-T-101-notification-panel-rebuild.md) | **The hierarchy was inverted, and that was the whole clutter problem.** The row set the *event* as a 600-weight title, so five consecutive rows read "Visit expired" while the only distinguishing fact — the factory — sat smaller and greyer beneath it. `StatusPill` now carries the event (tone + pulse + text, WEB-002 §5) and the factory is the title, so **the event label appears exactly once per row**. **Three duplications removed:** the date printed twice on every row older than yesterday (`dayHeading` *and* `relativeLabel` both fell through to `formatDate`); the unread count rendered as an identical chip on the trigger *and* in the panel header; and **the 56-string bell map existed verbatim in two files** — `Shell.tsx:162-218` and `features/shell/notification-strings.ts`, both using the two patterns WEB-013 bans outright. **"Mark all read" did not mark all read** — it looped the ≤15 loaded rows awaiting one UPDATE each, so 55 unread fell to ~40 in 15 round trips; now two statements. **Arabic relative time was grammatically wrong and no gate could see it:** `"قبل {n} ساعة"` interpolated a bare singular, so every count above two read as the singular — `Intl.RelativeTimeFormat` carries the plural per locale, so the template is gone and **no `locale === "ar"` branch was introduced**. **~85 lines re-implemented `MenuSurface`** badly — portal, placement, RTL edge, outside-click — while omitting Escape, focus trap, focus return and the height cap that was the owner's actual complaint; `role="dialog"` restores all of them. **No token was invented:** two reaches failed (`--sqx-menu-panel-w`, `--sqx-action-primary`) and were resolved inside the existing set (WEB-002 §2). `319 → 195` lines, a **232-line orphan `.module.css` with zero importers** deleted, `Shell.tsx` −57, baseline **733 → 704**, frozen-sheet classes **14 → 0**. **Owed:** signed-in render in Arabic first, axe, manual checklist, font-size count — the panel sits behind `/login`. |
| 2026-08-13 | T-100 · `/planning/workload` — capacity that measures the right thing, and the planning family finishes its Arabic | [2026-08-13-T-100-workload-capacity-and-arabic-sweep](sessions/2026-08/2026-08-13-T-100-workload-capacity-and-arabic-sweep.md) | **The screen spent six of ten columns on 5% of its data** — measured before touching anything: **2 of 42 active visits inside the six-week grid, 40 in "Later", 64 of 66 week cells zero**, and the column carrying 95% was an unlabelled catch-all. **"Relative utilization" was not a utilization, and the design system already said so**: `TrendBars`' TSDoc warns a series charted against its own maximum *"exaggerates a flat run"*, so **100% meant "has the most", not "at capacity"**, and it moved when someone else's load changed. **Comparing an all-time total to a per-day cap would have repeated the category error**, so the column is now **peak day vs the governed `daily_visit_cap`** — the top inspector reads **3 of 10**, not 100%. Weekly bars stay a *distribution* on a shared scale and are labelled as one; **no weekly cap was invented** (`dailyCap × 5` would be a governed value from thin air). `TrendBars` also fixed the zeros for free — **64 of 77 bars render `data-zero`** as a dashed baseline *"because an absence must never read as a small quantity"*, replacing 64 grey tracks. The capacity bar is `aria-hidden` (it duplicates adjacent text); distribution bars stay exposed because there they are the only encoding. **The banned `as unknown as` is gone, not relocated** — replaced by `isRecord`/`embedded`/`text` type predicates resolving the Supabase to-one embed once. **`cd-026` pins `WorkloadView.tsx` by path**; all five assertions re-verified **by script** against the rebuilt file, and its one governance comment kept knowingly (T-097's call). **Arabic sweep, found by measuring not reading** (DOM walk for Latin text while `lang=ar`): `/planning` 3 → 0 (`planning_lookups.label_ar` is seeded **NULL**, so `labelAr ?? labelEn` rendered English — the fallback now goes through `visits.enum`, database untouched), `/planning/map` 3 → 1 (`regionLabel` was **duplicated in two files and used in neither place that needed it**; extracted to `lib/ksa-regions.ts`), `/planning/workload` 10 → 0, `/planning/calendar` already 0 from T-098. **Localising the region broke `placeLabel`'s dedupe and that was mine** — the guard compared raw values, so `الرياض` stopped matching `Riyadh` and it rendered **"الرياض · Riyadh"**; fixed by deduplicating on source values, **caught by re-measuring, not by review**. Typography: **45 off-scale elements → 0**, six rendered sizes all on the nine-role scale, exactly one `display`; inline-styled 169 → 78 and **all 78 are token-valued custom properties**; legacy classes → 0; baseline **734 → 733**, locked in. **Owed:** axe, the English render, and **11 Arabic strings need native review**. **Parked:** `planning_lookups.label_ar` seeding; city names have no translation source; `MapView.tsx:75` still carries the banned cast; **WEB-014 §5.2 cites `saqeel/data/MetricStrip.tsx` as the reference implementation and that file itself violates the contract**. |
| 2026-08-13 | T-099 · the visit map — paging that lied, and a contract that froze the legacy | [2026-08-13-T-099-visit-map-migration](sessions/2026-08/2026-08-13-T-099-visit-map-migration.md) | **Done — e2e not run.** **The pagination was wrong before it was verified, and only rendering found it:** `.range(0, 24)` rendered **22 rows**, because PostgREST applies a filter on an embedded resource to the **embedding**, not the parent row, unless the join is `!inner` — so the coordinate filter filtered nothing and `count` counted unlocated visits. **`factories!inner` unconditionally** fixed page size and total together (**25 rows, `26–50 of 298`**). **`GeoMap` has carried `fitMarkers` all along**, its doc comment describing the exact "centred on whichever sorted first" bug on screen. **An entire column said one sentence per row** — "Unavailable under current scope" is now one note, and the column returns the moment any visit has a position. **`CoveragePanel`'s style contract was restated, not deleted, on the owner's authorisation:** it pinned the component to frozen-sheet globals and forbade importing any `.module.css`, so migration was impossible without changing the spec; the rewrite is **strictly stronger** — no inline `style`, no CSS-in-JS, **no string-literal `className` at all**, retired vocabulary named and banned, module asserted free of hex, px/rem and font declarations. **Three e2e literals pinned the file layout and were kept on purpose** (`from("geo_events")`/`official_lat`, `"latest inspector position"`, `` `/visits/${v.id}` ``, plus the `MappedVisit` export another spec imports) — **so the query could not move to `queries.ts`**. **Moving the filters above the map changed what they had to mean:** region/risk/window became URL state applied in the query, driving map, table and coverage together, which surfaced **two Region selects** on one screen (now one) and left `CoveragePanel` stateless — a **Server Component**. Window defaults to *any*, not 30 days, because the old panel default applied page-wide would have hidden every past visit. **A missing token stopped an invention** — no `--sqx-map-*` size token exists, so the canvas uses `aspect-ratio`; a map-height token is a change request. `trend-bars` restored the regional magnitude cue as one series chart rather than a bar per row. **Owed: `npm run test:e2e`**, axe, manual checklist. |
| 2026-08-12 | T-098 · `/planning/calendar` — the grid collapses, the enum stops reaching the screen | [2026-08-12-T-098-visit-calendar-grid-and-enum-labels](sessions/2026-08/2026-08-12-T-098-visit-calendar-grid-and-enum-labels.md) | **The rules are the container showing through 1px gaps, not borders on 42 cells.** Container paints `--sqx-border-subtle`, cells paint `--sqx-surface-default`, and the `gap: var(--sqx-border-width-hair)` between them *is* the rule; every row being the same `repeat(7, minmax(0,1fr))` aligns the vertical gaps into continuous columns. **No `:first-child` exceptions, no double borders, no physical direction** — correct in RTL by construction, not by override. **Not a new idea: the calendar catching up with `DataTable`**, which already collapses borders and bands its header, and whose own source argues square corners because *"rounding it would read as a floating chip rather than a header rule."* **Today had to change signal** — it was marked by `border-color` alone and cells now own no border, so it takes the `--sqx-surface-accent` fill `DataTable` gives a selected row. **`role="grid"` was a promise the markup did not keep** — the ARIA grid pattern implies arrow-key navigation, no cell was focusable, no roving `tabindex`; same class as T-094's malformed `role="tree"`. Now `table`/`row`/`cell`, **0 grid roles remaining**. **The raw label was three different fallbacks in three adjacent lines** — raw value for `planning_status` and `visit_type`, `.replaceAll("_"," ")` for `operational_state` — and since `getDict()` returns `{}` (T-086) **the fallback is the rendered string**, which is why every pill was lowercase, not just the underscored one. Per owner instruction labels now come from the locale JSON, not `ui_strings`: `visits.json` gained an `enum` block in **both locales, 22 keys each, parity 200 = 200**, covering `planning_status` (7), `operational_state` (8), `execution_mode` (3) and the `visit_type` reference values at their database definitions. **The Arabic was harvested, not invented** — `operations.json`, `enforcement.json`, `approvals.json` and a DEC-L seed already shipped 12 of them verbatim; planning/operational states take feminine agreement (الزيارة). **`humaniseEnum` is the fallback formatter and the TSDoc calls it a defect marker, not copy.** **Three surfaces wired, not one** — calendar, board and detail each had their own `t("enum.…")` closure, so fixing only the calendar would have created the inconsistency it was reported for. Measured live: per-cell border 1px → **0**, radius → **0**, grid frame → 1px + 12px, snake_case labels **4 → 0** (`pending_supervision` → `بانتظار الإشراف`), grid roles **2 → 0**, typography 734 → 734. **Owed:** axe, the English render (session locale is `ar` and `/en/…` does not flip the cookie), and **9 Arabic strings with no prior art need native review**. **Parked:** `factories/[id]` still uses `titleCase` so it renders "Pending Supervision" against the agreed sentence case; two more local `enumLabel` implementations remain; the day number is `Mono` where `DataTable` uses `tabular-nums`. |
| 2026-08-12 | T-097 · the visit calendar — a clean route that rendered English at 11.5px | [2026-08-12-T-097-visit-calendar-migration](sessions/2026-08/2026-08-12-T-097-visit-calendar-migration.md) | **Done — e2e not run.** **The route was certified clean and rendered English at 11.5px.** `/planning/calendar` reported 1 violation (the shell) while rendering `t-caption` (**11.5px**) ×5 and an inline `font: var(--type-caption-font)` (**12px**) per chip; **`CalendarBoard.tsx` was not in the typography baseline at all**, because the gate scans **CSS declarations** and the file had no CSS module. **This is T-091's hole — "all thirteen planning routes at 1" describes CSS, not screens.** **Every string was an English literal:** `visit.cal.*` existed in **neither** locale and `tr()` falls back to English, so Arabic users got an entirely English calendar; now a real `visits.calendar` namespace, 18 keys per locale, **verified rendering RTL in the browser** (يوم / أسبوع / شهر, `الخميس، 6 أغسطس 2026`). **Friday collapsed because `1fr` is `minmax(auto, 1fr)`** — `nowrap` chips with no `min-inline-size: 0` let one long factory name set its column's min-content. **Moving view/anchor to `?view=&on=` removed the client island**: `SegmentedControl` in **href mode** passes no handlers, so **the whole calendar is now a Server Component (`"use client"` 1 → 0)**, and deep links, back and refresh work. **Status left the `title` tooltip** for a `StatusPill` in all three views — it was previously colour-only and Day-view-only. **The e2e suite pins `CalendarView.tsx` by path** (`cd-026` `readFileSync`: `console.error`, `loadErrorNeutral`, `expire_lapsed_visits_scheduled`, no `{error.message}` in JSX), **which shaped the layout** — the board moved to `components/sections/`, the file did not, and the i18n key was named `loadErrorNeutral` deliberately. **One comment survives knowingly**: deleting the governance marker to satisfy the zero-comment rule would weaken an accepted behaviour. `CalendarBoard.tsx` marked `@retiring`, zero importers. Baseline **734 → 734**, none new. **Owed: `npm run test:e2e`**, axe, and the rest of the manual checklist. |
| 2026-08-12 | T-096 · `/planning/immediate` — declutter and a skeleton that mirrors the layout | [2026-08-12-T-096-planning-immediate-declutter](sessions/2026-08/2026-08-12-T-096-planning-immediate-declutter.md) | **Zero duplicated leaf text strings left in the form, measured in the DOM.** The registered-factory rule was rendering **three times** above the first field — `identity.r05Body` **verbatim twice** (top notice + `CardHeader description`) plus the disabled radio's `lockedType`; it now has one home, moved *inside* the identity card above the search field it governs. `identity.heading` was also rendering twice in one card (title + `<legend>`). **The identity mode radio group could not be operated** — `manualAvailable = false` was a literal, one radio `checked` with a no-op `onChange`, the other permanently `disabled` — and `actions.ts` **never reads `identity_mode`**, checked before the edit, so deleting it changes nothing submitted. **`DispatchProtections` rendered 9 chips and then re-listed every blocking chip verbatim in a notice below them**; it is now the `review-readiness` shape already shipped in `planning-bulk` — one pill, **blockers only**, each row focusing its own control. **Four of the nine chips were hardcoded `informational`** and could never change; `AUDIT`/`NOTIFY` had no control at all. Nothing was removed that is not still on screen: audit/notify → the "This will:" card, checklist/inspector → their own fields (the `CHECKLIST` chip **contradicted** its field, saying "select" where the field says optional), authorized → the access gate. **`WINDOW` now carries no focus target deliberately: `imm-window-start` has never existed** — `DateRangePicker` takes no `id` prop — so as a chip it was an invisible no-op and as a row it would be a dead button. **Zero new i18n keys**; 57 dead keys deleted per locale at asserted parity 940 = 940, including the whole `enforcement` block, which rendered nowhere and is never read by the action. **The empty space was the grid**: three cards of very unequal height in one `auto-fit` track gave `[Identity \| Urgency]` then `[Location \| ∅]`; Identity + Urgency now share a column, no new token, no media query. **The legacy loading state is off this route** — `RouteLoading` had hardcoded `en`/`ar` literals, the `locale === "ar"` ternary, `glyph="◫"`, `t-caption` at 11.5px, and a `<main>` nested inside `ShellClient`'s `<main>`; the new skeleton renders inside `Shell` so it is inset by the same `.sq-content` box as the page (**1017px wide, 20px either side, measured**), and `<main>` count is **1**. 27 `useState` → 14, 2 `useEffect` → 1, 9 comments → 0, typography 734 → 734. **The empty space took three rounds, and only the third measured before changing anything** — **780px of void** under the left column, **595px of it the inspection checklist**: 10 packages at `minmax(18rem, 1fr)` inside a 465px column resolve to **one** track, so ten options stacked. Moved to a full-width `dispatch-checklist/` card reusing `packageLabel`/`packageOptionalHint` as title and description (**zero new copy**), with `role="radiogroup" aria-labelledby` rather than a legend restating the title. The residual ~140px **cannot be closed by content** — Identity + Urgency is less than Location + six dispatch fields, and every redistribution moves the void to the other column — so the grid closes it: `align-items: stretch` + `.column > :last-child { flex-grow: 1 }`. **`flex-grow`, not `flex: 1`**, because `flex-basis: 0` makes the card's height depend on free space the row is sizing from that card and collapses it whenever the left column is taller. Urgency options also went **vertical** — four options across an 865px row is sparse for a required single-select. **Measured, Arabic, 1280px: left 712 → 909, right 1,492 → 909, void 780 → 0, protections 346 → 197, checklist 595@465 → 352@977, form 2,311 → 1,993** (before English / after Arabic, so the total is indicative; the void is structural). **Arabic/RTL passed:** `dir=rtl`, no horizontal overflow, no content clipping. **Owed:** axe and the rest of the manual checklist — the pane is displayed only intermittently and the route stalls in its Suspense boundary whenever it hides. **Open question:** a cold navigation shows `PlanningSkeleton` from the **parent** `/planning` boundary, which may mask every nested route's skeleton. |
| 2026-08-12 | T-095 · `/field` runs a second design system; its fonts came off Google | [2026-08-12-T-095-field-fonts](sessions/2026-08/2026-08-12-T-095-field-fonts.md) | **Done — fonts only; the scale is untouched and out of scope.** The owner supplied an inspector persona, unblocking `/field` for the first time since T-069. The sweep stopped at the first measurement. **`/field` is not an unmigrated SAQEEL surface — it is a different design system:** `app/(app)/field/layout.tsx:30` links `/saqeel-ds/saqeel/styles.css` from `public/`, which imports its own tokens and `components.css`, with a **thirteen-step** scale (28 · 22 · 17 · 15 · 14 · 13 · 12.5 · 12 · 11.5 · 30) — none of them SAQEEL's nine, and the source of every off-scale size there. **Migrating 38 files while that sheet redefines `--font-*`/`--type-*` for the whole subtree would be work against a live override**, fixing neither the fonts nor the scale, so it was raised for a ruling rather than started (WEB-002 §1); owner ruled fonts only. **The real defect was a runtime dependency on Google Fonts:** `tokens/fonts.css` is an `@import` of `fonts.googleapis.com`, while `app/layout.tsx` deliberately self-hosts via `next/font/local` **with the reason written in the file** — the build must not depend on a Google fetch that fails in restricted environments. **The field channel therefore carried a network dependency the rest of the application had already removed, on the surface most likely to be offline, and one that registers a PWA.** `var(--font-plex-arabic)` now leads `--font-body` (both `:root` and `[dir=rtl]`) and is appended to `--font-mono` before the generic; the literals stay as fallbacks. **`--font-mono` deliberately keeps `"IBM Plex Mono"` first** — a genuine monospace for machine identifiers is a *design* decision, not a font defect — so Latin identifiers render exactly as before while Arabic glyphs inside them stop falling to an arbitrary OS face. **Two measurement corrections, both mine:** `document.fonts.check()` reports the `FontFaceSet`, **not whether text renders**, and returned `false` for `"IBM Plex Mono"` — I wrongly reported 16 elements rendering in a missing font, when a real span layout showed `as declared 247.50 == "IBM Plex Mono" 247.50` against `ui-monospace` 209.84; and a canvas `measureText` probe **silently mis-parses a quoted family**, which is what made the wrong claim look confirmed. **Measure by laying out a span with the element's own computed properties, never by canvas.** Family census **IBM Plex Sans 129 · Mono 16 · plexArabic 97 → plexArabic 221 · Mono 16**; counted violations **226 → 226** (the fix is in `public/`, which the gate does not scan); type scale unchanged. **This is a visible change and not neutral:** the faces differ — `IBM Plex Sans` 157.30 vs self-hosted `IBM Plex Sans Arabic` **175.23** on identical text, ~**11% wider** across 129 elements — defensible on WEB-014 §2.0 ("one typeface") and it removes the offline risk, but recorded as a design change, not a repair. **Checked at tablet 768px for the consequence T-069 warned about:** no horizontal page scroll, **0 clipped elements**, one overflow in the **shell** (out of scope). **A DO-NOT-CHANGE note sits at the top of `tokens/typography.css`** for whoever reworks that design system. **Parked, and it is the real question:** `/field` is **226 counted + 502 gate-invisible** (t-caption 329 · id-code 164 · t-label 9) across **36 routes** — either the `<link>` goes and `/field` inherits SAQEEL, or `/field` is declared a separate system and its 226 leave the baseline. **Until then the baseline overstates what this programme owns by roughly a third.** |
| 2026-08-12 | T-094 · `/planning/bulk` — the same four facts, said four times | [2026-08-12-T-094-planning-bulk-declutter](sessions/2026-08/2026-08-12-T-094-planning-bulk-declutter.md) | **Partial — the screen body was never rendered.** Ten stacked blocks at first load → **five**, with **no fact removed**: every deletion was a second copy of something still on screen. `view.eligible` reached the screen **four times** (criteria footer, ledger, toolbar badge, pager) → **once**; the "no criteria" copy **three times** → once, two of them sharing a sentence verbatim between `notice.noCriteriaBody` and `empty.noCriteria.body`; the risk advisory twice, differing by one word. **The ledger's denominator/eligible/excluded triple was left alone** — `excluded` looks like a fourth duplicate but CD-021 is explicit that the three together are the screen's signature; reading the file is what stopped that deletion. **The five "Not available" rows were already in the dropdown** (`fieldChoices` sets `disabled` + `note`), so they collapsed into one native `<details>` reusing `factory-sections`' marker. **Pager, campaign summary and toolbar badge are gated, not deleted** — they mount when they have something to say. **A permanent info pill announced a failure that never happened:** `DRAFT_PERSISTENCE_EXECUTABLE = false` made `draftPersistable` always false, so the `draftSaveFailed` string rendered on every load at zero selection. **`role="tree"` was malformed** — a bare `<span key={i}>` between `role="group"` and `role="treeitem"` broke the owns-relationship and there was no roving `tabindex`, so it announced as a tree and behaved as a list; now plain nested lists, and the `<form>` finally carries `aria-labelledby`. **Match became radios, and the radio was already built** — `components/saqeel/choice` takes `kind="radio"`, is token-driven and has 9 call sites, so nothing new was built for the control. The *group* was missing: four modules had each hand-rolled the same `<fieldset>` + `<legend>` + `<Text role="label">`, so **`components/saqeel/choice-group` is a promotion past the Rule of Two, not an invention**. It also fixes an accessibility defect invisible while the control was a segmented button — the old markup wrapped it in `Field`, and a `<label>` names one control, never a set. `name` carries `pathKey(path)` or nested criteria groups fuse into one radio group. **A third `RadioGroup` exists in the legacy `saqeel/inputs/` family — zero usages, inline `style`, `--space-*` tokens, retired `t-caption` — parked, not used.** **`PlanningNotice` was putting a `<div>` inside a `<p>`** — it rendered `children` inside `<Text>` (a `<p>`) and the select-all confirm notice passed a `Field`, so the notice was a **hydration error**, found from a real Planner session. **Fixed at the component, not the call site:** an `actions` slot renders as the paragraph's sibling and the TSDoc states that `children` takes phrasing content only — otherwise the trap stayed armed for the other 23 consumers. All were audited; `Choice` (`<label>`) and `<strong>` elsewhere are phrasing content and legal, so this was the app's only invalid one. **The advisory then moved to the top and became a strip:** `/planning/bulk` is now the **third consumer of `AdvisoryStrip`** (after `dashboard/executive-brief` and `factories/factory-ai-advisory`) through a 39-line wrapper in the same shape — one flex row instead of `Card` + `CardHeader` + `CardBody`. `AdvisoryStrip` gained one **optional** `unavailable` string, and only because it renders `result.error` raw while `generateContextualInsight` returns untranslated English prose; dashboard and factory pass nothing and are byte-identical. **`AiAdvisory` was deliberately not retired** — it looks orphaned, but `ContextualAiPanel` (itself `@retiring`, 6 downstream consumers) imports it and the ledger names it that migration's `replaced-by`. **That leaves two AI advisory components and no ruling on which is canonical — raised, not decided.** Baseline **734 → 734**, none new. **Owed: the session is signed in as Inspector**, so only the unauthorized state rendered (which did confirm the header change); axe, the manual checklist and the Arabic render all need a Planner or Supervisor session. |
| 2026-08-12 | T-093 · `/planning/visits` — and the planning family closes | [2026-08-12-T-093-planning-visits-typography](sessions/2026-08/2026-08-12-T-093-planning-visits-typography.md) | **Done — the populated board was not rendered.** 15 declarations across 3 modules → **0**, including **5 retired `caption` refs**. **With this, all thirteen planning routes sit at 1 violation — `NotificationBell.tsx:270`, the shell, and nothing else.** Baseline **749 → 734**. **The pressed row's reference goes 700 → 600, deliberately:** `.preview[aria-pressed="true"]` set `font-weight: var(--sqx-weight-bold)` (700), but the scale has **no 700 at body size** and `bodyStrong` (600) is the only emphasis role for body text, so the selected row now renders `Text role={active ? "bodyStrong" : "body"}`. This is the **mirror image of T-087 and T-091**, where KPI values moved 600 → 700 because `metric` *is* bold — the same correction (a weight assembled by hand is not a role), with the direction set by the scale rather than a preference. **`.preview` already had `font: inherit`, and that was checked rather than assumed:** it is a `<button>`, so an absent declaration would have meant Arial (T-064), and the gate flagged only the `aria-pressed` state — which could equally have meant the base was silently broken. It was not, so no Arial defect existed and none was introduced. **Two `<input>` got `font: inherit` and nothing else needed it** — `<label>`, `<a>`, `<ul>` and `<p>` inherit `font` normally and simply lost their declarations. **`--sqx-status-critical-on-soft` was reused as `tone="danger"` on the strength of T-091's token check** (both resolve to `--sqx-error-darker` / `--sqx-error-light`), so `.formError`'s colour could be deleted rather than preserved on a wrapper; the class keeps its padding and background and became a `<div>`, which now matches its own `useRef<HTMLDivElement>` focus target, with `role="alert"` and `tabIndex={-1}` unchanged. **T-091's invisible-violation sweep ran first and came back clean** — no `.t-*` legacy classes, no string-literal `className` anywhere in the tree — that check is now part of the route inventory rather than an afterthought. **The selector→element map's first pass reported every class as "(not found)"** because the classes live in **sibling** files (`visit-spine.tsx`, `visit-table.tsx`, `visit-bulk-actions.tsx`) rather than the namesake component; resolved by grep before proceeding — **a "not found" from tooling is a question, not an answer.** Measured live: **one typeface, zero off-scale**, filter bar 12px ×13 and 11px ×1, search `<input>` plexArabic 14px/22.4px/400 confirming `font: inherit` worked. **Owed, and it is most of the diff:** `main` held the loading skeleton for most of the pass, so the **spine, bulk-action forms, eligibility list and table body — where 10 of the 15 declarations were — were never rendered**, and **the pressed state was never seen**, so `bodyStrong` on a selected row is asserted from the scale rather than observed. **Parked:** the 700 → 600 change wants an owner's eye, since it reduces emphasis on the one element telling a planner which row the spine is showing (if too weak, the answer is a second signal, not a heavier weight). |
| 2026-08-12 | T-092 · `/planning/immediate` — clean by the gate, two typefaces on screen | [2026-08-12-T-092-planning-immediate-typography](sessions/2026-08/2026-08-12-T-092-planning-immediate-typography.md) | **Done — one file changed, and the baseline did not move, which is the point.** The route reported **1 violation** (the shell) and the tree was **genuinely clean**: three greps came back empty — no `.t-*` legacy classes (T-091's hole), **no string-literal `className` at all**, and no typography declaration in any CSS under `app/(app)/planning/immediate/` or `components/sections/planning-immediate/`. The measured render still showed **two typefaces**: `© Mapbox`, `© OpenStreetMap` and `Improve this map` in **Helvetica Neue**, injected by `mapbox-gl`'s own stylesheet — **the same defect T-074 found on `/operations/live`**, in a component no source file in this route references. **T-074's fix was real but reached two files, not the app:** its record claims the shared chrome module means *"any future map inherits the same chrome"*, which is true of `operations-map-panel.tsx` and `LiveMapInner.tsx` and **only** those two — **`GeoMap`, which has 18 consumers, was never wired up**, so every map in the application outside operations has rendered Helvetica Neue attribution since T-074. **Fixed at the component, not the route** — one import plus `.chrome` on both className branches of `GeoMap` — because per-screen would have fixed one of eighteen and duplicated a hack, the same argument T-074 itself used when it refused to copy the per-route `:global()` block. **The change had to preserve a load-bearing invariant:** `GeoMap` carries an eleven-line comment explaining that `mapboxgl-map` must appear in *every* value React writes, since Mapbox adds it at construction, React overwrites the attribute on the next render, and losing it drops the `position: relative` that the absolutely-positioned canvas resolves against — "the map paints over the entire page". Both branches keep it, and it was **verified in the DOM rather than assumed**: `position: relative`, and the canvas rect **exactly equal** to the container rect (385 × 254 at y=352). Attribution now renders **plexArabic 12px / 600 = `label`**, reusing T-074's ruling that attribution is `label` not `body` so the fix does not enlarge legally-required fine print. Baseline **749 → 749, unchanged — the defect was never in the count.** **Owed:** 16 of the 18 GeoMap surfaces are not individually verified (`/field/*` needs an inspector persona per T-069). **Parked:** `GeoMap`'s own unavailable state renders `<p className="t-caption">` (11.5px, T-091's invisible class) and `<span>⌖</span>` (glyph-as-icon, rule 8) — not provoked here because the map loaded, and T-072's rule says error states must be provoked, not assumed. **General lesson recorded: a shared-component fix must be verified at the component, not at one call site** — there is no gate for "component X composes module Y", and T-074 recorded a claim about "any future map" that was not true. |
| 2026-08-12 | T-091 · `/planning/bulk` typography — and a hole in the gate | [2026-08-12-T-091-planning-bulk-typography](sessions/2026-08/2026-08-12-T-091-planning-bulk-typography.md) | **Done.** 19 route-owned declarations across 7 modules → **0**, plus **4 rendered elements the gate could not see**. **The gate cannot see a legacy global type class applied from JSX, and this route proved it:** `/planning/bulk` reported **1 violation** — the shell — while still rendering **four elements at 11.5px**, off every scale. The source is `DistributionPanels.tsx` using `className="t-caption"`, a class defined in the **frozen** `tokens.css` (`--type-caption-size: 11.5px`). Every gate rule scans **CSS files for declarations**; the frozen sheets are exempt by design and a `className` in a `.tsx` matches no rule at all, so the count and the render disagreed and **only measuring found it**. `t-caption` appears in **162 `.tsx` files** repo-wide — **a route can be at "1 violation" and still be off-scale.** **`--sqx-status-critical-on-soft` and `--sqx-text-danger` are the same value — checked, not assumed:** both resolve to `--sqx-error-darker` in light and `--sqx-error-light` in dark, so `tone="danger"` is exact and `ai-advisory`'s `.error` could be deleted outright rather than given T-090's keep-the-colour-on-a-wrapper treatment. **A colour token outside the tone list is not automatically a different colour.** **`.tree, .notice` is a grouped selector and the selector→element map only reported the first class** — the font applied to both; both are mixed-content flex rows so they inherit, per T-065. **`<bdi>` has no primitive** and cannot be dropped (bidi isolation for factory codes in Arabic), so its declaration was deleted rather than composed — the inherited render is identical, the same reasoning already applied to `<td>`. Two KPI values moved **600 → 700** (`Metric`), the same deliberate correction as T-087. **No native controls in this set at all**, unlike T-090 — verified by the same selector→element map. Measured before and after: **30 · 28 · 20 · 16 · 14 · 12 · 11.5 with 4 off-scale → 30 · 28 · 20 · 16 · 14 · 12 with 0 off-scale**, one typeface, 309 leaf nodes. Baseline **768 → 749**. **Twelve of the thirteen planning routes now sit at 1 violation — the shell**; only `/planning/visits` remains at 16 and it owns none of them. **Three mistakes, each caught by a check rather than by reading:** a missing `Text` import that compiled into the **DOM `Text` interface** and failed with *"cannot be used as a JSX component"* — silent in the gate, obvious in `tsc`, and it caught the same omission in a second file; two more unbalanced `</span>` from wrapper conversions; and `.filterStatus` nearly losing its `inline-flex` layout, caught because the **unused-class check** flagged it, which prompted re-reading the block — **a class that becomes unused after a migration is a signal to re-read it, not to delete it reflexively.** A scripted class-removal also matched **0 of 4** patterns (CRLF, third occurrence) and reported success; switched to per-block edits immediately. **Parked:** `BulkForm.tsx` is **dead** — zero importers, ~240 lines of legacy globals, a complete parallel implementation of this screen (check the T-077 spec-as-text trap before deleting); a gate rule matching `className=".*\bt-(caption\|body\|label\|meta\|micro)\b"` in `.tsx` would close the hole above, raised not added; and `DistributionPanels.tsx` still renders legacy structural globals (`panel-header`, `sq-grid-2`, `stack`) — a rebuild, not a typography task. |
| 2026-08-12 | T-090 · `/planning` typography — the largest pocket | [2026-08-12-T-090-planning-typography](sessions/2026-08/2026-08-12-T-090-planning-typography.md) | **Done — the visit drawer, 10 of the 45, never rendered.** 45 declarations across 11 modules → **0**; `/planning`'s only remaining violation is `NotificationBell.tsx:270`, the shell. **Four native controls got `font: inherit`, and only four.** `<input>`, `<button>`, `<select>` and `<textarea>` do **not** inherit `font`, so deleting a declaration from one renders the UA's Arial (the T-064 defect) — two inputs and two buttons took `font: inherit`. **`<a>`, `<label>`, `<summary>`, `<th>` and `<td>` are not in that set** and were **checked rather than assumed**: every selector was mapped **selector → rendered element by script** before any edit, and that map is what found the four. Treating the safe five as controls would have added four needless lines; treating the four as ordinary elements would have shipped Arial on the search box and the visit-reference button. **The primitives take no `className`, and that shaped every edit** — `Text`/`Heading` expose no class hook, so a class mixing layout with typography cannot become a primitive; the rule applied throughout was *the element keeps its layout class, the primitive goes inside it as a span*, which is why `.pagerLink`, `.trigger`, `.fieldValue`, `.status`, `.advisory` and both `<th>`/`<button>` cases still carry a class (layout only) while **13 purely-typographic classes were deleted outright**. **`--sqx-accent-ai` is not one of the nine tones, so the colour moved rather than changed:** `planning-assistant`'s heading is `#A78BFA`, and mapping it to `tone="accent"` would have changed a colour during a typography migration — exactly what T-065 forbids — so it is now `Heading tone="inherit"` wrapping a `.headingInk` span; verified live at `rgb(167, 139, 250)`, byte-identical to the token. **Table cells inherit, column headers do not:** `.cell` holds mixed content (pills, links, text) and keeps colour only, per T-065's mixed-content precedent, while each `<th>` label became `Text role="label"` — and the first `<th>`, which holds the select-all checkbox and no text, correctly got no primitive. `planning-denied`'s `<h1>` became `visual="heading"` because its level and appearance genuinely diverge, leaving `planning-header` as the route's single `display`. Rendered and measured: **30 · 28 · 20 · 14 · 12, five sizes, zero off-scale, one typeface**; h1 30/34.5/700 display, h2 20/26/600 heading, th and summary and pager link 12/16.8/600 label, the visit-reference button 14/22.4/600 bodyStrong — **`font: inherit` confirmed working, no Arial**. Baseline **813 → 768**. **Three mistakes recorded for the next session:** an unbalanced `</span>` — T-069's exact mistake one task after reading it, caught by re-reading the file because **the gate reads CSS and stays green on broken JSX**; a `className={undefined}` written reflexively on `Text`, which has no such prop and would have dropped the `<dd>`'s alignment; and a scripted bulk replace that matched **0 of 7** patterns because the file is CRLF and **reported success on an unchanged file** — a zero-match bulk edit is the T-058/T-076 signal to change approach, which is what happened. **Owed:** the visit drawer opens on a row click and `drawerCount` was 0 in the measured DOM, so its `h2`, `subtitle`, five `groupTitle`s, `dt`/`dd` and `openLink` are unverified; e2e needs a production build. **Parked:** `planning-visit-table` is still a hand-rolled `<table>` rather than `DataTable` (T-076's fifth instance) — its typography is now right but it inherits none of `DataTable`'s fixes. |
| 2026-08-12 | T-087 · `/planning/bulk/review` typography | [2026-08-12-T-087-bulk-review-typography](sessions/2026-08/2026-08-12-T-087-bulk-review-typography.md) | **Done — but nothing was render-verified, and that is the task's real gap.** 30 declarations across 9 modules → **0**; the route had never been swept and its only remaining violation is `NotificationBell.tsx:270`, the shell. **The whole app renders body text at two line-heights, and this route had both.** `<body>` is matched by **two** `font:` shorthands — `saqeel.css:869` (`--sqx-text-body`, 1.6 → 22.4px) and `saqeel-runtime.css:19` (`--type-body-font`, 1.5 → 21px) — and **the frozen legacy sheet wins on load order**, so the design system's own body rule loses. The twelve `font-size: var(--sqx-text-body-size)`-only classes here were therefore rendering the body *size* with legacy *leading*; measured before the change, `review-standby`'s `.note` was **14px/21px** while the already-migrated `PlanningNotice` on the same family was **14px/22.4px**. Migrating them adds **+1.4px of leading per line** — toward the documented contract and consistent with every migrated surface, recorded rather than shipped silently. **KPI values change weight 600 → 700, deliberately:** `.value`/`.cellValue` set `font-size: var(--sqx-text-metric-size)` with `--sqx-weight-semibold` — metric *size* at the wrong *weight*, because the class was assembled from parts instead of using the role — and `--sqx-text-metric-weight` is bold, so `<Metric>` renders 700, which is what WEB-014 §2/§5.2 require of every number. **Two focusable headings were a genuine §11.4 primitive gap:** both need `subheading` *plus* a `ref` *plus* `tabIndex` because `ReviewClient` focuses them after publish succeeds or fails, and `Heading` accepts neither while `Text` has no `subheading` role at all. The owner was asked whether to extend `Heading` and did not answer, so the **reversible** option shipped — a `<div tabIndex={-1} ref>` wrapper around the `Heading`, a pattern already used in four places in this repo (`DraftEditor`, `PublishControls`, `VisitsBoard`, `DecisionPanel`) — and **it also fixes a pre-existing accessibility defect**, because `review-outcome`'s `<h3 role={live}>` was **not a heading in the accessibility tree**: `role="status"` replaces the heading role. Three `useRef<HTMLHeadingElement>` became `HTMLDivElement` to match. **`<legend>` hit the same wall as T-088** and took the same answer (element stays, `<Text as="span">` inside) — **second instance; the third should extend the `as` union rather than repeat the workaround.** `review-standby.module.css` **deleted** (its only class was `.note`); `review-context`'s `.moment` was **dead** — 17 lines including a `:focus-visible` block, orphaned when the window control became `DateRangePicker`. Baseline **843 → 813**, no orphaned `styles.x` and no unused class in either direction. **Owed:** the route shows standby without a staged bulk plan and the session **expired mid-check** (`?reason=expired`, the T-072/T-074 failure), so every claim about rendered output is derived from `type.module.css`/`saqeel.css` rather than observed — the `Metric` weight change and the leading change both want a measured after-state. **Parked:** the `<body>` line-height conflict is app-wide and needs a ruling on load order; `review-eligibility` shows seven counts at two treatments (six `Metric` 28px, the eligible one a 14px `StatusPill`), which WEB-014 §2 rule 2 forbids but is a design question, not a typography violation. |
| 2026-08-12 | T-089 · `/factories` declutter — one AI strip, one identity, one provenance card | [2026-08-12-T-089-factories-declutter](sessions/2026-08/2026-08-12-T-089-factories-declutter.md) | **Done — axe, Arabic, light theme and e2e owed.** Owner reported *"a lot of clutterness"* and asked for the AI part to work like the dashboard version. **The clutter was measured before anything moved:** the workspace rendered **11 cards, 8 h2, 21 status pills, 131 leaf text nodes and 3,039px for ONE factory**, with **14 distinct strings appearing more than once** — the factory name 3×, `Plant number` 3×, `Petrochemical` 3×, `Open violations` 3×, and *"This section is available in the full factory profile."* **4×**, because `FactorySections` rendered it inside each of four `<details>` instead of once for the group. **The dashboard had already fixed the AI defect and it was never carried across:** `FactoryAiAdvisory` was a 226px Card printing the idle line, the provenance line **and** the no-confidence line unconditionally above an *empty* result — exactly what T-060 removed when `ExecutiveBrief` became a strip whose notes render *with* a brief. **Rather than copy the strip and create the second implementation this programme keeps paying for** (T-071 live map, T-076 hand-rolled table), it was extracted to `components/ai/advisory-strip/` and both surfaces compose it; two orphaned stylesheets went with it and the two components that each held their own `useActionState` now share one. **Owner ruled the action sits below the paragraph** — the dashboard's `margin-inline-start: auto` was what created the gap — which **grows the dashboard brief 44 → 74px**, stated rather than hidden; if it should keep the inline action the strip needs a variant, not a fork. A first cut set `visual="bodyStrong"` on the shared heading, which would have shrunk the dashboard title 20px → 14px and disturbed a route T-059 closed at four sizes — **caught and reverted before shipping, then measured at 20px**. **One governed figure was rendering twice**, the same defect T-060 recorded as *"two names for one governed figure"*: `81.5` and its `Critical attention required` pill in both the snapshot and Risk outlook. The snapshot keeps value and band, the outlook keeps only the explanation, and its `Open factory profile` button went too as the **third** route to `dossier_href` (T-068's duplicate-entry-point rule). **`latestChange` became `string | null` rather than a sentence meaning "none"** — its fallback repeated the Risk trend empty state word for word, so returning `null` removes the duplication by construction while keeping the line when there is real movement. Two provenance cards (*Source trust*, *Source status & freshness*) answered one question and merged; the selected-context card was **deleted outright** because name, CR, licence and plant already render in the hero and the rail, and the rail's check mark already shows the selection. **Zero spec pins existed on any factories card**, checked first; the dashboard brief is pinned only by heading name in two specs, both verified still resolving. `cards 11 → 9 · pills 21 → 19 · leaf nodes 131 → 116 · workspace 3,039 → 2,512px · AI 226 → 74px · outlook 390 → 268 · provenance 382 → 278 · duplicated strings 14 → 9 · 4 dead locale keys · 140 keys per locale at parity`. **The 9 remaining repeats are the left-rail-versus-hero overlap and were deliberately kept** — the rail is for scanning a portfolio, the hero for reading one factory. |
| 2026-08-12 | T-088 · `/planning/single` typography | [2026-08-12-T-088-planning-single-typography](sessions/2026-08/2026-08-12-T-088-planning-single-typography.md) | **Done — 3 of 5 components never rendered.** 8 route-owned declarations across five modules → **0**; the route's only remaining violation is `NotificationBell.tsx:270`, the shell, excluded by owner instruction. Every change was a **layer** move, not a size change: the replacement was verified declaration-by-declaration against `type.module.css` **before** editing — `.text` supplies `margin: 0`, `[data-role]` supplies `font` + tracking + `text-wrap: pretty`, `[data-tone]` supplies the exact colour token the class used — which is why **four classes were deleted outright** (`.body`, `.label`, two `.hint`) rather than trimmed. **`.legend` carried a redundant declaration that reads like a size change in the diff:** it set `font: var(--sqx-text-label)` *and* `font-weight: var(--sqx-weight-semibold)`, but the `label` role's weight **is** semibold, so the second line was a no-op. **`Text` cannot render a `<legend>`** — its `as` union has no such member — and rather than extend the union (a design-system change the owner asked to be consulted on), the `<legend>` keeps the element and gains a `<Text as="span" role="label" tone="secondary">` child; phrasing content is legal there and the fieldset association is untouched. **Parked: if a third site needs it, extend the union with agreement.** **`planning-notice` is shared by four surfaces**, so the fix also removes a violation from `/planning/bulk`, `/planning/bulk/review` and `/planning/immediate` — and it is the only one of the five that renders outside the wizard, so it is the one that could be measured: `<p>` preserved, **14px / 22.4px / 400**, colour `rgb(196,205,213)` = `--sqx-text-secondary`, `margin-top: 0`, one typeface — identical to the deleted CSS. Baseline **851 → 843**, re-baselined. **All eight declarations sat on plain text** (`<p>`, `<span>`, `<legend>`) — checked before editing, so T-064's *"deleting a font-size from a control makes it Arial"* trap did not apply. **Owed, and stated plainly:** `visit-configuration` (both legends, both hints), `identity-dossier` and `portfolio-picker` sit behind steps 2–4 of a four-step wizard that **would not hydrate past the loading skeleton** in this pane (the T-061/T-072/T-082 compositing failure); the Factory 360 handoff URL was tried and did not help. `<legend>` is confirmed **absent from the DOM**, so that change is unrendered and unmeasured — WEB-014 §11.3 wants it, and the next session on this route should drive the wizard to step 3. |

| 2026-08-12 | T-084 · `/visits/[id]` actions card on SAQEEL controls, split into `visit-actions/*` | [2026-08-12-T-084-visit-actions-on-saqeel-controls](sessions/2026-08/2026-08-12-T-084-visit-actions-on-saqeel-controls.md) | **Done — the 3 rewritten e2e interactions are unverified, and that is the real risk of this task.** Owner asked for the design system's own select and input on the Management actions card, plus the split. **The Arabic planner was reading English and the helper was one file away:** `ActionBar` rendered `{o.label_en}` for every governed return and cancel reason, while `features/visits/detail/view.ts:24` already exported `reasonLabel()` picking `label_ar` by locale, and `field/inspection/[id]/results/page.tsx:70` had been doing exactly that all along — **the third recorded instance of T-079's "search for the helper before writing the fallback".** Options are now built in the view model so no component can reach a raw column; **selecting a translated DB column by locale is a data read, not the `locale === "ar"` copy ternary WEB-013 bans.** **The banned native `<select>` survived T-080 because `Select` cannot POST** — it is a `<button>` plus listbox — so it gained `name` (hidden input carrying the value), `defaultValue` and `required`; **swapping without `name` would have compiled, rendered correctly and silently submitted an empty field on every governed transition.** `defaultValue` is the load-bearing half: an uncontrolled control means **the forms hold zero `useState`** (WEB-004 §1 rung 4, read once from `FormData`), and net state versus `ActionBar` is **−2**. `required` writes `aria-required` only — a hidden input is exempt from constraint validation — so enforcement stays on the server where it already was for all five fields; **no capability is lost, but it raises the cost of the parked hardcoded-English action copy**, since an Arabic user now meets those strings more often. **The reschedule window needed no new component and the owner was right that one existed:** `DateRangePicker` already carried `withTime`, `timeStep` and `timeLabels` and already emitted `YYYY-MM-DDTHH:mm`, so both `datetime-local` inputs are gone with nothing built — it only needed `nameFrom`/`nameTo`. The old code read `toISOString().slice(0,16)`, the **UTC** instant, so editing a window would have written it back three hours early — the T-076 defect class **on a write path**; `riyadhLocalInput()` joined `lib/dates.ts` because that is where `TIME_ZONE` lives. **Migrating to a listbox breaks `selectOption()`, unavoidably:** three spec call sites drove the native control and now click the trigger and pick the row by label, the reassign helper reading **the same roster RPC the page reads** so spec and UI cannot disagree; two `form >> button` locators were narrowed to `button[type=submit]` because the form now contains the trigger button too and the old ones would raise strict-mode violations; and `cd-027:97` read `ActionBar.tsx` as **source text**, re-pointed to the new shell before the file was deleted (the T-077/T-078 `readFileSync` trap). **A blank listbox was a regression I introduced and had to fix** — this environment seeds zero return reasons, the native control still rendered `—` while the listbox opened onto an empty panel that reads as a failure to load; `Select` gained `emptyLabel` and now renders *No options are configured* as a disabled option (rule 9). The required marker also left the resource files, where `"Return reason *"` had a screen reader announcing *"Return reason star"*. **Two dead keys deleted** (`cancelReason`, whose `#visit-cancel-reason` assertion was already passing vacuously, and `executionStarted`, superseded by `scheduleLockedWhy`), and the blocked row stopped listing the three locked actions twice. `ActionBar.tsx` **257 → deleted**; `visit-actions/` is 12 files, largest 153, **at the directory cap**. Verified live: `natives: 0`, `dateInputs: 0`, hidden `reason_key`/`inspector_id` in the payload, rows aligned 507/501 (was 90\|265\|65 and 65\|265\|77), overflow 0. **Two parallel-implementation finds:** `components/saqeel/inputs/` is a **dead input layer inside the design system** — 8 of 9 files at zero importers with names colliding with the live components, so "use our own Select" can import the dead one built on the banned native control — and `DateRangePicker.DEFAULT_STRINGS` is **English copy inside a primitive**, which is what the shell renders today. **Owed:** e2e (Playwright browsers are not installed here), axe, Arabic (still blocked on the locale toggle), light theme, 320px. |
| 2026-08-12 | T-085 · `/planning` method filter — six values that never match | [2026-08-12-T-085-planning-method-filter](sessions/2026-08/2026-08-12-T-085-planning-method-filter.md) | **Done.** The Method filter rendered **ten options, six of them broken**. `planning-screen.tsx:97` read `Object.entries(messages.methods)` as a value→label map; `methods` actually held **nine** keys — three real method values *and* six title/description strings for a card picker. Selecting *"Plan bulk visits"* set `?method=bulkTitle`, which reached the database unvalidated (`queries.ts:59` did `first(sp.method) \|\| undefined`, no whitelist) and hit `.eq("visit_plans.method", …)` at `visit-list.ts:258` — returning **an empty list with no error**, reading as "no visits match" rather than "that is not a filter". **One object was serving two incompatible contracts and only one was true:** `planning-drafts.tsx:38` uses the same object correctly as `methods[draft.method]`. **Fixed at two layers, because either alone leaves the hole open** — cleaning the JSON would have made `Object.entries` correct *by coincidence*, and the next key added re-breaks it; options now derive from a canonical `PLANNING_METHODS` and the URL parameter is whitelisted against the same constant. **The whitelist pattern already existed three lines up:** `tab` was validated against `PLANNING_TABS` in the same function while `method` was passed straight through — the fix is the existing pattern applied to the field that was missed. `PLANNING_METHODS` went beside `PLANNING_TABS` in `visit-list.ts`, which already owns the planning list constants and is where the filter is applied. **`features/planning/create-methods.ts` deleted** (33 lines) — its `planningCreateMethods()` was the only consumer of the six dead strings and was **never called**; WEB-006 §4's three conditions all cleared, including the T-077 trap (no spec reads it as text). 12 dead i18n strings removed, **no new copy authored in either locale**, parity 1011 = 1011 identical. Measured on the dev server via `Showing N of M`: no filter **101**, `?method=bulkTitle` **0 → 101**, `?method=bulk` **8** and `?method=single` **1** both unchanged. **New defect found and parked, needing a ruling: `?method=immediate` can never match** — `rowSelect`/`countSelect` switch the `visit_plans` embed to `!inner` when a method filter is set, while line 340 *derives* the label `"immediate"` from `visit_plans` being **NULL**, so a visit displayed as Immediate is precisely one the join excludes; it is now **more** visible, not less. **Measurement note: the first measurement was wrong** — a loose regex reported the unfiltered list as 2 visits when the real total is 101, caught only because a *filtered* result (8) exceeded the supposed unfiltered total, an impossibility that forced a re-measure. **When a number contradicts an invariant, re-measure before explaining it.** **Owed:** e2e (needs a production build). |
| 2026-08-12 | T-083 · design-system retired-role floor | [2026-08-12-T-083-design-system-retired-role-floor](sessions/2026-08/2026-08-12-T-083-design-system-retired-role-floor.md) | **Done.** 12 declarations across 8 `components/saqeel/*` stylesheets, every one a single token rename — `caption → body`, `code → mono`. **The fix had to live inside the design system and that is not a contradiction:** WEB-014 §4.1 makes `components/saqeel/` the only legal authoring zone for typography, so a retired role referenced there is unreachable from anywhere else. The owner approved on the explicit split that **`app/saqeel.css` and `components/saqeel/type/` stay untouched** — no token added, removed, renamed or revalued — while the component stylesheets change. A future session must not read "design system untouched" as covering `saqeel/*/*.module.css`. **Equality was proven at the token, not inferred from a size histogram:** `--sqx-text-caption-{size,line,weight,tracking}` are each declared `var(--sqx-text-body-*)` (saqeel.css:609-612) and the composites at 796/787 differ only in which alias they read, so the swap deletes an indirection rather than changing a value. **The Arabic case was the one that could have broken it, and it was checked rather than assumed.** `:lang(ar)` overrides `--sqx-text-body-line` to `1.8` while `--sqx-text-caption-line` is `var(--sqx-text-body-line)`; custom properties substitute at computed-value time, so had `lang="ar"` sat on `<body>` while the aliases were declared on `:root`, caption would have computed `1.6` against body's `1.8` and this rename would have **silently lengthened every affected line in Arabic**. `layout.tsx:72` puts `lang` on `<html>`, which *is* `:root` — verified live: with `locale=ar`, `--sqx-text-caption-line` reads `1.8` and `caption === body` compares `true`. StatusPill measured **14px/22.4px/600 en** and **14px/25.2px/600 ar**, exactly `body × 1.6` and `body × 1.8`, one typeface both locales. `/factories` and `/factories/[id]` **7 → 1**, `/planning/visits/[id]` **8 → 1**, `/planning/immediate` **8 → 2**, `/planning` 52 → 46, `/planning/bulk/review` 41 → 32; the residual 1 on every otherwise-clean route is `NotificationBell.tsx:270`, the shell, not the route. Gate 863 → 851, re-baselined, delta exactly 12 so no concurrent agent's work was absorbed. **Found, not fixed:** `sq-notification__badge` renders **10px**, below WEB-014 §7's 11px floor, in a frozen sheet on every route. **`npm run lint` and `npm run verify` do not exist** in `apps/web` or at the repo root, though CLAUDE.md's working protocol names both. **Owed:** e2e (needs a production build). |
| 2026-08-12 | T-082 · `/visits/[id]` write-surface layout, upload preview, duplicate copy | [2026-08-12-T-082-visit-detail-write-surface-layout](sessions/2026-08/2026-08-12-T-082-visit-detail-write-surface-layout.md) | **Done — Arabic/RTL render owed, and blocked outside this diff.** Owner-reported on T-081's output: the notes textarea is a sliver, the uploader is an orphan on its own line with a screen's width of dead space beside it, and a chosen file shows no preview. **One declaration caused both symptoms, and it was T-081's own fix.** `align-items: flex-start` on `.stackedForm`/`.uploadForm` sets the **cross** axis of a **column** flex container, so every field collapsed to intrinsic width — measured on the live route, textarea **178px** (the `cols=20` UA default) inside a 444px form, drop zone **229px** (its longest text line). T-081 added it so the submit would not stretch; it fixed the button and broke every field above it. **A container-level alignment is not a way to size one child** — the container is now `stretch` and the button sits in its own `.formActions` row, keeping its natural width because it is `inline-flex`. **`repeat(auto-fit, minmax(min(18rem, 100%), 1fr))` — the `min()` is load-bearing, and only measurement said so:** plain `minmax(18rem, 1fr)` gives a 288px track floor that a 183px container cannot honour, so the zone overflowed its own grid at narrow widths — a 320px reflow failure invisible in source and to every gate. **`auto-fit` is also what makes the empty state correct, which is why no media query exists:** with nothing chosen the surplus track collapses to 0 and the zone spans the row (the "orphan with dead space beside it" the owner objected to); with a file chosen the preview claims column two and both are equal — 780|780 at 1600px, ~1340px of dead space → 0. **The blob preview needed no `useEffect` and leaks nothing:** the object URL is created in the change handler and revoked in `<img onLoad>` once the bitmap has decoded, with the element keyed by the URL, so WEB-004 §3's disposal case never had to be opened — verified live, `src` scheme `blob`, image still painted, and a `fetch` of that URL now fails. **Write-path integrity proven rather than reasoned about:** after a scripted `DataTransfer` selection the native input still reported `files.length === 1`, so the server action's `FormData` read is untouched; no action signature, form `name`, hidden identity field, `required` flag or guard moved, and `cd-027-visit-detail.spec.ts` pins none of this copy or these selectors. **Three statements of one permission fact became one** — the Notes hint, the `fileLabel` parenthetical and the empty state each said *planner or operations only*, and `Attachments` now carries it once as the `CardHeader description` it was the only card of the pair to be missing. `noFileChosen` deleted: a resting "No file chosen yet." under a zone reading "Drag a file here / or browse your device" restates the zone. **`notes.saving` and `att.uploading` deleted as dead copy in both locales** — they were mapped in `strings.ts`, declared on two prop types and rendered by nothing, because **`Button.busy` keeps its label by documented design**; check what a primitive already promises before translating a string for it. **One deliberate deviation from the approved mockup:** the filename lives in the always-mounted `live="status"` line below the grid rather than inside the preview panel, because the alternative was a live region that mounts with its content (unreliable to announce) or the filename twice; `aria-describedby` is conditional so it never points at an empty description. **Owed:** the Arabic render — `/ar/…` and the shell's `ع` toggle both left `lang="en"`/`dir="ltr"`, **a locale-switching defect outside this diff that blocks every route's WEB-011 pass**; CSS was asserted logical-property-only with no `[dir]` override and en/ar parity exact instead. Also owed: axe, 200% zoom, light theme, e2e. **Measured in headless Chrome against the real CSS modules** because the Browser pane repeatedly stopped compositing (all rects 0 — the T-061/T-072 failure); **Playwright's browsers are not installed on this workstation**, so `npm run test:e2e` cannot run here at all. |
| 2026-08-12 | T-078 · repair the already-broken responsive spec | [2026-08-12-T-078-repair-the-broken-responsive-spec](sessions/2026-08/2026-08-12-T-078-repair-the-broken-responsive-spec.md) | **Partial — one spec repaired; the deletion-enabling re-pointing not started.** The task was to re-point the assertions blocking three orphan-tree deletions, and checking every `read()` path for existence **first** surfaced something more urgent: **the suite is already broken, and not by this programme.** `responsive-dashboard-operations.spec.ts` reads `operations/live/live.module.css` and `operations/live/LiveOps.tsx`, both deleted during the T-070/T-071 live-ops rebuild; its `read()` is a bare `readFileSync`, so **two tests throw before a single assertion runs**, and `git log` on the spec returns nothing — it was never touched by that work. That is precisely the failure mode T-070's own record described ("a spec that names a file rots when the behaviour moves"), and **the rebuild that produced the lesson missed one of its own call sites**. **The live claims turned out to be already re-pointed — in a different spec:** `web-admin-m3-operations.spec.ts:302-312` carries them with the reasoning attached, including the deliberate decision **not** to carry the old `[dir="rtl"]` override across because WEB-002 §6 forbids one, asserting logical properties instead. So the correct repair was **to cross-reference, not duplicate** — a claim asserted in two specs makes both weaker, since either can drift while the other still passes, and **de-duplicating a re-pointed claim is not dropping it**. The live reads were removed with a comment naming the spec that owns the contract. **`providerFailed` and the locale contract did need genuine re-pointing**, being claims about the live component rather than its stylesheet, and now read `components/operations/operations-live/operations-live.tsx`, verified to contain both. **Every assertion was checked by script against the real files — 13/13 pass** — per T-070's rule that a re-pointed assertion is verified, never eyeballed; the single "MISSING" the verifier printed was its own regex catching the `"utf8"` argument of `readFileSync` as a path. **A further finding: the dead dashboard tree is bigger than recorded.** `RevampStrategicView.tsx` is imported **only** by `DashboardView.tsx`, which has zero importers — a second closed dead pair after T-077's `assistant-view.ts` cycle, so the tree is `DashboardView`, `DecisionCanvas`, `RegionalScope`, `BasisDrawer`, `RevampStrategicView` and `dashboard.module.css`. **Not started, and deliberately so: the deletion-enabling re-pointing across 7 spec files.** Those assertions are governance claims — `"Compliance in approved inspections"`, `"approved inspections only"`, `href="/reports"`, `"Open records"` — each of which must be traced to its equivalent on the shipped `strategic-view`/`operational-view` surface, and **some may no longer be the right claim**, with the `[dir="rtl"]` case as precedent. It is a contract task that wants a suite run to confirm, and rushing it alongside a deletion would be the wrong call. **Blocked on:** a production build to run the suite (WEB-005 §8, the human's). |
| 2026-08-12 | T-081 · `FileUpload` drag-and-drop primitive | [2026-08-12-T-081-file-upload-primitive](sessions/2026-08/2026-08-12-T-081-file-upload-primitive.md) | **Done — a real drop was not exercised.** Owner-reported: the visit-detail file field was still a raw `Choose File / No file chosen`, and *Save notes* sat flush against its textarea. **The native input is the control; the zone is only its clothing** — T-080 had just recorded that `Select` cannot participate in a form because it has no `name`, and building an upload the same way would have repeated that defect on a **write path**, so `<input type="file">` is kept, visually hidden but never replaced, with the drop zone as a `<label htmlFor>`: click-to-browse works natively with no key handler and no `ref.click()`, and the keyboard works because focus lands on the real input. **A drop must hand its `FileList` to that input, and that is the one imperative write** — `inputRef.current.files = dataTransfer.files` is WEB-012's sanctioned library handoff, since there is no React way to populate a file input and without it a dropped file would display and then submit nothing; **the rule bans DOM writes that substitute for render, not the handoff a browser API requires.** The hidden input keeps its focus ring by moving it to the zone via `:focus-within`, and the selection line is both `aria-describedby` and `role="status"`. **The gap defect was structural, not a spacing token:** `<form>` is a block, so the button had zero gap — a form is a column of fields, so `.stackedForm` gives it `flex-direction: column` and one gap token, now 12 px verified in the DOM. 2 registry icons added; 4 keys in both locales at parity 147. **Owed:** a real drag-and-drop was never performed — click-to-browse is proven structurally, not by opening a picker — and **4 new Arabic strings need review**. |
| 2026-08-12 | T-077 · delete the dead planning tree | [2026-08-12-T-077-delete-the-dead-planning-tree](sessions/2026-08/2026-08-12-T-077-delete-the-dead-planning-tree.md) | **Done — but only 1 of the 4 orphan trees could go, and finding that out was the substance of the task.** The owner asked for a deletion task covering four dead trees and 72 typography violations; reading WEB-006 §4 first showed that its second condition — *no dynamic import, no string-referenced path, no test fixture references it* — **fails for three of the four**. `DashboardView.tsx` is read as **source text** by four spec files (`execution-crossmodule-contract`, `insp-717-reports-index`, `responsive-dashboard-operations` twice, plus a comment in `wcag-inspector-field-audit`); `dashboard.module.css` by two; `FactoryList.tsx` and its stylesheet by two more. **Deleting a file that a spec reads as text does not fail the type checker — it fails the suite at runtime when `readFileSync` throws**, which is invisible to `tsc`, invisible to the typography gate, and invisible to any grep for `import`. **A fourth "dead tree" turned out not to be dead at all, and that is a correction to my own T-072 record:** `operations.module.css` is still imported by the live `OperationsPreview`. What is actually true is narrower — all 49 of its typography declarations sit on classes only the dead `operations-details.tsx` uses — so the file cannot be deleted and only `operations-details.tsx` can, which is itself named in `web-admin-m3-operations.spec.ts`. T-072's record has been amended in place. **The one deletable tree was larger and more tangled than the parked note claimed:** the tracker recorded 7 dead components but the directory holds 10, `planning-skeleton` is **live** (imported by `/planning/loading.tsx`) and was kept, and three of the others were imported **for types only** by `features/planning/assistant-view.ts` — which is itself imported only by `planning-recommendations`, which is dead. **That closed dead cycle reads as "still referenced" to a naive import search**, and was only resolved by checking whether anything *outside* the candidate set imports those types. **A name collision nearly cost a live file:** `planning-assistant` exists in both `components/planning/` (live, edited in T-076) and `components/sections/planning/` (dead) — deleting by basename would have removed the wrong one. Result: **9 files deleted, 38,809 bytes (~38 KB), 30 violations, repo 893 → 863**, with 9 rows added to the retirement ledger under *Retired*. Verified by per-directory import search across `src`/`e2e`/`scripts`, a clean typecheck, and `/planning` SSR returning 200 with 552 KB of content and **no `Module not found`** — checked against the server response because the pane would not finish streaming. **Owed:** WEB-006 §4 also requires a green e2e suite on the replacement routes, which needs a production build. **Also noted: none of the deleted files carried the `@retiring` banner the rule requires, and `gate:retirement` does not exist in `package.json`** — so the banner/ledger pairing WEB-006 §4 describes is currently unenforced, alongside the already-parked missing `lint`. **Three orphan trees remain (~42 violations), all blocked on re-pointing their spec assertions — and per T-063's lesson, re-point the assertion, do not delete it.** |

Fixed to `subheading` (16px) over `body` (14px), with `subheading` chosen on WEB-014 §2's own wording — "a named group inside a card" is exactly what an empty state is, whereas `body-strong` is defined as emphasis inside body, not a title. `.description` was also still sitting on the **retired `caption`** role and moved to canonical `body`. **The methodological point worth keeping: a size-only audit would have missed this entirely.** Both 12px and 14px are on-scale, so nothing would have flagged; the defect only appears when a title is compared against its *own* description, which means **dumping weight and colour alongside size**. The change cost the route no extra size, because 12px appeared on this screen only as that title. **The session check added in T-074 earned its place immediately** — the route hung on its `loading.tsx` fallback mid-task, and fetching it returned 369 KB with the board present and no login markup, proving the session was alive and the pane was at fault this time; a fresh tab rendered it. **The same symptom has now had two different causes in two consecutive tasks, so the check should always be run rather than guessed.** **Owed, and it is the majority of the screen: only the empty state was observable.** This Planner sees no open exceptions, so the populated board — groups, rows and counts — never rendered. Baseline diff audited entry-by-entry — **all 41 removals are this task's own files**, none absorbed from the concurrent session. Converting the markup to `Heading level={2}`/`level={3}` cost no more, and moves the route toward its scheduled rebuild instead of adding debt to it; **all 18 `<h2 id>` kept their ids, so every `aria-labelledby` target and the whole document outline are unchanged** — the outline is simply now expressed visually as well as semantically. Result: **0 off-scale sizes (was 4), 9 → 7 distinct sizes, one typeface.** **Scope was held deliberately and the owner was told so:** `page.tsx` is **409 lines** against WEB-001’s 40-line route ceiling with the entire screen inline, and `factory360.module.css` (269 lines) sits on the **pre-Saqeel `--type-*` token set** — a different design system — alongside frozen `.sq-surface`/`.sq-numeric`/`.sq-caption` globals. **This task made the route render correctly; it did not make it correct.** Two stragglers remain, both inside `ContextualAiPanel`, which already carries an `@retiring` banner naming this route as a blocker and whose replacement exists — styling a retiring component is work that gets deleted. **Verification note: this route takes ~40–50s to render in dev** and the Browser pane sits on the `loading.tsx` fallback throughout, while a `fetch()` of the same URL returns the full 419 KB in 3.6s; fronting the tab and waiting is what lands it, so **do not conclude the page is broken from the fallback alone**. | 2026-08-11 | T-065 · `/factories` primitive migration (part 2 of 2) | [2026-08-11-T-065-factories-primitive-migration](sessions/2026-08/2026-08-11-T-065-factories-primitive-migration.md) | **Done — rendered and measured after every batch.** 20 components migrated off feature-CSS typography onto the type primitives; **`components/sections/factories/` is now at zero violations**, scope 76 → 11 and none of the 11 on `/factories`. Two stylesheets deleted outright once migrated, because they held nothing but typography. **The migration found three primitive gaps that the inventory had not, and each would have caused a silent defect if ignored.** `dir="auto"` was unavailable on `Text` and nine call sites carry it on user-supplied data — factory names, coordinates, provenance lines — where in an Arabic-first app it is correctness rather than decoration: without it a mixed Arabic/Latin string renders in the wrong visual order, on exactly the strings most likely to be bilingual. `role="alert"` collided with `Text`s own `role` prop, which names the typography role, so an explicit `live` prop was added instead of renaming `role` across every existing call site. And `Heading` could not express a heading that renders small — it offered only display/heading/subheading, while two real headings need `body-strong` and `label`. **A headings semantic level and its visual weight are independent; that is the entire point of the `visual` prop, so it has to cover the whole scale, not just the large end.** **Two near-misses, both caught by measuring rather than reading:** converting the trend `<h3>`s to `subheading` moved them 14px → 16px and quietly added a **fifth** size to a route that had just been brought to four; and a regex strip removed `font: var(--sqx-text-metric)` from the portfolio KPI values with no primitive replacing it, which would have shrunk every portfolio number from 28px to 14px — fixed with `Metric tone="inherit"` inside the existing `<dd>` so the status tone colours stay exact, because **colours must not change while migrating typography**. **One bad trade was caught on review:** the portfolio summary was an `<h2 id>` serving as the cards `aria-labelledby` target, and the first pass converted it to `<Text as="span">` — gate-clean, but it **removes a heading from the document outline**. Reverted, then done properly once `Heading visual="label"` existed. **A violation count is never worth a real heading.** Mixed-content containers — flex rows of pills, links and badges rather than text nodes — keep no font declaration and inherit `body`, giving the same rendered result without wrapping every fragment. `font: inherit` on the portfolio button is deliberately kept and is not a violation; it is T-064s Arial fix. `FactoryList.tsx` + `factory-list.module.css` confirmed **dead** (zero importers) and routed to retirement rather than migrated. Every heading on the route now computes on-scale at 12/14/20px — before this task the portfolio `<h3>` computed to the UA default 17px while its text rendered at 14px in a child span. **Owed:** axe, 320px, Arabic/RTL. |
